Korrekturen am Inventar von heute Mittag

  • CORRECTED — suite size. HANDOFF.md line ~44 and the prior inventory both carry '1223 passed, 35 subtests'. Measured today: `python -m pytest --collect-only -q tests` reports **1399 tests collected** across **86 files**. The delta is the newest work, which the inventory never saw.
  • CORRECTED — docs/FEATURE_INVENTORY.json cites 72 of the 86 test files and never mentions the other 14, which are the newest and among the largest: test_council_bus, test_council_canary, test_council_publish, test_council_session, test_council_vendors, test_spine_attempt, test_spine_attempt_containment, test_spine_cancel, test_spine_ledger, test_spine_picker, test_room_summarize, test_room_wiring, test_stream_hook, test_offload_automint. Together those are ~460 of the 1399 collected tests — a third of the suite is invisible to the prior inventory.
  • CORRECTED — inventory entry 'daedalus CLI dispatcher / status: wired / tests: [tests/test_wires.py] / notes: 26 subcommands'. Three errors: (a) test_wires.py imports only `daedalus.ikarus_os` and never touches cli.py; (b) `cli.main()` is invoked by ZERO tests — the only executions are `cli._council([...])` in test_council_session.py:652 and a source-text grep in test_spine_picker.py:672; (c) the count is 29, not 26 (`grep -c 'cmd == "' daedalus/cli.py` → 29).
  • CORRECTED — inventory entry 'SSE event stream (project events) / GET /api/events / tests: [tests/test_web_api.py]'. test_web_api.py never references /api/events. No test in the suite opens any SSE stream.
  • CORRECTED — inventory entry 'SSE Ikarus token stream / GET /api/ikarus/stream / tests: [tests/test_ikarus_stream.py]'. That file tests `daedalus.ikarus_os`'s stream function with urlopen mocked; it never imports web_api and never dispatches the route.
  • CORRECTED — the general shape of the inventory's API-endpoint test citations. Of 19 GET routes in web_api.py, exactly TWO are ever reached by a test: /api/dashboard (test_ui_contract, live threaded server) and /api/latent/search (test_web_api, hand-built handler object). Every other route's 'tests:' citation points at a test of the underlying FUNCTION, not the route.
  • VERIFIED — inventory entry 'Vector index auto-embedding on append / status: dark / DAEDALUS_VECTOR_INDEX=1'. Correct: the gate exists and test_embeddings.py exercises the embedder without it.
  • VERIFIED — inventory entry 'Compat shims for the kairos rename / status: shim' covering daedalus/{ikarus,decompose,drafts,mission_control,orchestrate}.py. Read all five: each is a pure re-export of daedalus.kairos.*. None has a direct test, which is fine for a shim, but note the citation of tests/test_mission_control.py is loose — that file imports `daedalus.kairos.control` directly, not the shim, so a broken re-export would not be caught.
  • STALE DOCSTRING — tests/test_fenrir_slice_attack.py header says 'Both are failing tests against the current tree.' They pass now (both are in the 1399 collected and the suite is green). The docstring should be re-worded to 'both were confirmed failing before the fix'.
  • STALE DOCSTRING — tests/test_wires.py lines 25-29 say two tests in test_ikarus_os.py and test_ikarus_stream.py still use `codex_cli` as their 'unwired provider' example and 'will need a follow-up'. That follow-up has landed: test_ikarus_os.py:41 and test_ikarus_stream.py:164 both now use `provider='gemini'`. The NOTE should be deleted.
  • CORRECTION to my own earlier read: daedalus/adapters/transport.py appeared in a naive 'no test imports this' scan, but tests/test_adapters.py imports `InMemoryTransportSink` from the `daedalus.adapters` package re-export, so transport.py IS covered. Reporting it as untested would have been the confident-wrong-entry failure mode.

Offen / unklar

  • ZERO tests for these production modules — no test file imports them at all (AST-verified across all 86 files): daedalus/providers/claude_cli.py (the ClaudeCLIProvider, i.e. the ESCALATION lane, reached from providers/__init__.py:112), daedalus/providers/personas.py (imported by codex_cli, deepseek, ollama, provider_router and kairos/scheduler — wired and untested), daedalus/providers/_report.py, daedalus/benchmark.py, daedalus/runbook.py, daedalus/langgraph_adapter.py, daedalus/eval/__main__.py, daedalus/structcore/__main__.py, daedalus/memory/__main__.py, daedalus/structcore/metrics.py (only ever exercised transitively through build_index). The claude_cli one is the sharpest: the lane every escalation lands on has no test.
  • ZERO executable tests for the entire JavaScript/TypeScript surface. `find apps -name '*.test.*' -o -name '*.spec.*'` returns nothing; there is no test script in apps/web/package.json's test position and no runner in vscode-agent-env/. The React app (apps/web/src/App.tsx, api.ts, components/, hooks/) and the VS Code extension (vscode-agent-env/extension.js) are covered only by Python source-text greps in tests/test_comms.py and by the shape contract in tests/test_ui_contract.py.
  • structcore-rs: 12 `#[test]` blocks, ALL in src/clones.rs. index.rs, languages.rs, parse.rs and main.rs have zero. There are no Python↔Rust parity fixtures anywhere in tests/ (only a passing mention in test_honest_denominator.py:126), so docs/bypasses.md §5's 'structcore-rs parity lags' is not measured by anything.
  • No test opens an SSE stream, no test issues a POST or PUT to web_api, and no test drives the room's web GUI (runs/council/room_server.py is only import-checked and signature-checked by test_room_wiring.py:466-505).
  • daedalus/kairos/evolution.py and daedalus/kairos/shadow_shell.py have ZERO production callers (grepped the whole repo, excluding tests and build/) — tests/test_kairos_evolution.py protects an island, entirely with MagicMocks.
  • daedalus/hermes/ contains nothing but __pycache__/ — an empty package directory that survives, with no tests and no references anywhere in the codebase.
  • No coverage measurement of any kind. There is no `coverage`/`pytest-cov` invocation, no .coveragerc, no reported percentage. Every 'thin coverage' judgement in this map (including mine) is by inspection.
  • No automated mutation testing, and therefore no enforcement of the round-2 rule the HANDOFF sets ('every guard kept must have a test that goes red when that specific guard is disabled, verified by actually disabling it'). It has been done by hand for exactly two files.
  • No conftest.py means five cheap safety nets are missing: an autouse DAEDALUS_CACHE_DIR redirect (closes the host-cache pollution), a socket-blocking fixture (would have caught test_ui_contract's unmocked live Ollama probe), a rootdir-independence fixture (closes test_web_api's CWD-relative Path() reads), marker registration, and a free-port fixture for test_ui_contract.
  • What I could not determine and what I would need: (a) whether runs/council/stream_hook.py is actually installed as a Claude Code hook — I would need to read .claude/settings.json / settings.local.json; (b) which of the 1399 collected tests currently SKIP on this machine — I would need to run the suite, which I was instructed not to do (the static gates are 14 skipif/skip markers plus 19 skipUnless conditions across 8 files, of which the tree-sitter, tiktoken and git conditions are all satisfied here and the os.name=='nt' ones are satisfied on Windows); (c) whether any test is currently RED — the 1223-green figure is stale by ~176 tests and I have not re-measured it.

Oberflächen (6)

  • `python -m pytest -q` from C:\Users\nukei\Desktop\agent_env — the only way this suite runs. 86 files, 1399 tests collected, ~7.9s just to collect.
  • `python -m unittest discover tests` — several files still document this in their docstrings (test_build.py, test_dynamic.py, test_hardening.py) and every unittest-style file has an `if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()` footer, so single-file runs work.
  • Direct single-file execution, e.g. `python tests/test_build.py` — supported by the unittest footers, unsupported for the ~25 pytest-style files (bare `def test_` + fixtures).
  • No CI surface at all: `.github/workflows` does not exist. bench_run.ps1 runs pytest on the TCT_app project over on the bench, not on this repo.
  • No coverage tooling, no mutation tooling, no lint config for the tests themselves.
  • Test-adjacent runtime surfaces the suite starts on its own: a real daedalus web_api HTTP server on fixed port 127.0.0.1:8796 (test_ui_contract), a stdlib HTTPServer on an ephemeral loopback port (test_ollama_native), real git repos in tmp_path (test_worktree, test_spine_*, test_eval_mint, test_temporal_ceiling, test_churn), real `sys.executable` subprocesses (test_council_vendors, test_spine_cancel, test_fenrir_slice_attack), and real Windows junctions via `cmd /c mklink /J` (test_worktree, test_spine_attempt_containment).

Erzeugt aus einer Tiefenlesung des Repositories durch neun parallele Leser plus eine Synthese. Jeder Statuseintrag trägt Pfad und Aufrufer; wo ein Leser unsicher war, steht „unklar“ statt einer Vermutung. Diese Karte ist eine Momentaufnahme und altert — sie ersetzt nicht das Lesen des Codes, sie sagt nur, wo man anfangen muss.
